### direct-ja

```
--- step 1: user says: 一言で：TCP と UDP の一番の違いは何ですか？
  [partner message] TCPは「信頼性を重視する」、UDPは「速さを重視する」通信方式です。

TCPは届いたか確認して再送し、順番も守るので確実に届きます。UDPは確認や再送をしないぶん軽くて速いですが、届かないことや順番が入れ替わることがあります。
```

### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Usa rebase para ordenar tus commits locales antes de compartirlos, como traer tu rama al día con la principal y dejar un historial lineal. Usa merge cuando la rama ya está publicada o la usan otras personas, porque reescribir su historial con rebase les causaría problemas.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 是可靠的、面向连接的传输，保证数据按序、不丢不重地到达；UDP 不做这些保证，只管尽快发出去，换来更低的延迟和开销。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase when the branch is still yours alone and you want a clean, linear history before sharing it, like tidying up before a PR. Merge when others have already pulled or built on the branch, since rewriting shared history causes trouble for them.
```

### direct-zh-howto

```
--- step 1: user says: bun test 怎么只跑一个测试文件？
  [partner message] 直接在 `bun test` 后面跟文件路径就行：

```bash
bun test src/utils/parse.test.ts
```

只想跑某个测试用例，用 `-t` 按名字过滤：

```bash
bun test -t "解析空字符串"
```

也可以只写文件名的一部分，Bun 会匹配到对应的测试文件：

```bash
bun test parse
```
```

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log is an append-only file where a database records each change before applying it to the main data files. After a crash, the database replays that log to recover committed changes that hadn't reached the data files yet, so writes stay durable without rewriting data pages on every commit.
```

})();

  [an app card under the message: made "hawdb write benchmark", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 1665 characters): <main class="k-app">
  <div class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">Latest night</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="w"><span class="k-none">Waiting for the first run</span></div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="s">Writes per second · p99 latency · compaction stalls</div>
  </div>
  <h2 class="k-h">Nightly runs</h2>
  <div class="k-card">
    <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
  </div>
  <div class="k-empty" id="empty" hidden>Each night's run lands here after 23:00.</div>
</main>
<script>
function render(runs) {
  var list = document.getElementById('list');
  var empty = document.getElementById('empty');
  list.innerHTML = '';
  if (!runs || !runs.length) { empty.hidden = false; return; }
  empty.hidden = true;
  runs.slice().reverse().forEach(function (r) {
    var row = document.createElement('div');
    row.className = 'k-row';
    var main = document.createElement('div');
    main.className = 'k-row-main';
    main.textContent = r.night + ' · ' + (r.writes / 1000) + 'k writes/s';
    var sub = document.createElement('div');
    sub.className = 'k-row-sub';
    sub.textContent = 'p99 ' + r.p99 + ' ms · compaction stalls ' + r.stalls;
    row.appendChild(main);
    row.appendChild(sub);
    list.appendChild(row);
  });
  var latest = runs[runs.length - 1];
  document.getElementById('w').textContent = (latest.writes / 1000) + 'k writes/s';
  document.getElementById('s').textContent = latest.night + ' · p99 ' + latest.p99 + ' ms · compaction stalls ' + latest.stalls;
}
function load() {
  Promise.resolve(ducter.get('runs')).then(function (v) {
    render(v && v.runs ? v.runs : []);
  });
}
ducter.onChange(load);
load();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"runs":[{"night":"Night 2","writes":181000,"p99":4,"stalls":0},{"night":"Night 3","writes":182000,"p99":4.1,"stalls":0}]}

  <div class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">北京出差 合计</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="total"><span class="k-none">还没记</span></div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">记了第一笔，合计就会显示在这里</div>
  </div>

  <div id="days"></div>

  <details class="k-add" id="addbox">
    <summary>记一笔</summary>
    <div class="k-field">
      <label for="fday">日期</label>
      <input class="k-input" id="fday" placeholder="11-23">
    </div>
    <div class="k-field">
      <label for="fwhat">花了什么</label>
      <input class="k-input" id="fwhat" placeholder="午饭、打车、酒店…">
    </div>
    <div class="k-field">
      <label for="famt">金额（元）</label>
      <input class="k-num" id="famt" inputmode="decimal" placeholder="0">
    </div>
    <button class="k-btn primary" id="save">记下</button>
  </details>
</main>
<script>
(function () {
  var items = [];
  var openId = null;

  function esc(s) {
    return String(s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, function (c) {
      return { '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;' }[c];
    });
  }
  function money(n) {
    return '¥' + (Math.round(n * 100) / 100).toLocaleString('zh-CN');
  }
  function sumOf(list) {
    return list.re … length + ' 笔,合计 ' + money(sumOf(items))
        : '还没记');
    }
  }

  function load(v) {
    var parsed = [];
    try { parsed = typeof v === 'string' ? JSON.parse(v) : (v || []); } catch (e) {}
    items = Array.isArray(parsed) ? parsed : [];
    render();
  }

  document.getElementById('fday').value = today();

  document.getElementById('save').onclick = function () {
    var day = document.getElementById('fday').value.trim();
    var what = document.getElementById('fwhat').value.trim();
    var amt = Number(document.getElementById('famt').value);
    if (!day || !what || !isFinite(amt) || amt <= 0) return;
    items.push({
      id: String(Date.now()) + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 6),
      day: day,
      what: what,
      amount: amt
    });
    document.getElementById('fwhat').value = '';
    document.getElementById('famt').value = '';
    document.getElementById('addbox').open = false;
    save();
  };

  if (window.ducter) {
    Promise.resolve(ducter.get('items')).then(load, function () { load(null); });
    if (ducter.onChange) ducter.onChange(function () {
      Promise.resolve(ducter.get('items')).then(load);
    });
  } else {
    render();
  }
})();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {}

    <div class="k-sum-label">合计</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="total">¥0</div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="count">还没记</div>
  </div>
  <h2 class="k-h">北京出差花销</h2>

    <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
  </div>

<script>
function fmt(n){return "¥"+Number(n).toLocaleString("zh-CN");}
function draw(){
  const xs = ducter.get("expenses") || [];
  const total = xs.reduce((s, x) => s + Number(x.yuan || 0), 0);
  document.getElementById("total").textContent = fmt(total);
  document.getElementById("count").textContent = xs.length ? xs.length + " 笔" : "还没记";
  const list = document.getElementById("list");
  list.innerHTML = "";
  for (const x of xs) {
    const row = document.createElement("div");
    row.className = "k-row";
    const main = document.createElement("div");
    main.className = "k-row-main";
    main.textContent = x.what;
    const sub = document.createElement("div");
    sub.className = "k-row-sub";
    sub.textContent = x.day;
    const val = document.createElement("div");
    val.className = "k-row-value";
    val.textContent = fmt(x.yuan);
    const wrap = document.createElement("div");
    wrap.appendChild(main);
    wrap.appendChild(sub);
    row.appendChild(wrap);
    row.appendChild(val);
    list.appendChild(row);
  }
  ducter.summary(xs.length + " 笔,合计 " + fmt(total));
}
ducter.onChange(draw);
draw();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"expenses":[{"what":"高铁","yuan":662,"day":"2026-11-18"},{"what":"酒店两晚","yuan":1036,"day":"2026-11-18"},{"what":"午饭","yuan":268,"day":"2026-11-19"}]}

### app-prs-known-en

```
(earlier) user: which of my PRs are still waiting on someone?
(earlier) partner: Four: wey-gu/ducter#412 (compaction cut) waits on Weizhen's review since Monday; nowledge-co/mem#10287 (retry on 502) on CI, failing; nowledge-co/hawdb#1592 (flaky compaction test) on Alice since Tuesday; wey-gu/ducter#418 (apps bar) is a draft.
--- step 1: user says: make that a little page I can keep and tick off as they land
  [partner message] Done. It's a page with your four PRs. Tick each one off as it lands, and the count at the top drops.
  [an app card under the message: made "PRs waiting", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 2313 characters): <main class="k-app">
  <div class="k-sum">
    <span class="k-sum-label">Still waiting</span>
    <span class="k-sum-value" id="count">4</span>
    <span class="k-sum-sub">Tick each one off as it lands</span>
  </div>

  <h2 class="k-h">Your PRs</h2>
  <div class="k-card">
    <ul class="k-list" id="list"></ul>
  </div>
</main>

<style>
  .k-row { display: flex; gap: 12px; align-items: flex-start; }
  .k-row input[type=checkbox] { margin-top: 4px; width: 18px; height: 18px; accent-color: var(--d-accent); flex: none; }
  .k-row-main { flex: 1; min-width: 0; }
  .k-row.done .k-row-main { opacity: .5; text-decoration: line-through; }
  .k-row-sub { color: var(--d-muted); font-size: 13px; margin-top: 2px; }
  .k-row-value { font-size: 12px; color: var(--d-soft); white-space: nowrap; }
</style>

<script>
  var items = ducter.get("items") || [];

  var landed = {};

  function render() {
    var list = document.getElementById("list");
    list.innerHTML = "";
    var open = 0;
    items.forEach(function (it) {
      var done = !!landed[it.id];
      if (!done) open++;
      var li = document.createElement("li");
      li.className = "k-row" + (done ? " done" : "");

      var cb = document.createElement("input");
      cb.type = "checkbox";
      cb.checked = done;
      cb.addEventListener("change", function () {
        landed[it.id] = cb.checked;
        if (window.ducter) window.ducter.set("landed", landed);
        render();
      });

      var main = document.createElement("div");
      main.className = "k-row-main";
      main.innerHTML = '<div>' + it.title + '</div>' +
        '<div class="k-row-sub">' + it.repo + ' · ' + it.waiting + '</div>';

      var val = document.createElement("span");
      val.className = "k-row-value";
      val.textContent = done ? "Landed" : it.status;

      li.appendChild(cb);
      li.appendChild(main);
      li.appendChild(val);
      list.appendChild(li);
    });
    document.getElementById("count").textContent = open;
  }

  if (window.ducter) {
    window.ducter.get("landed").then(function (v) {
      landed = v || {};
      render();
    });
    window.ducter.onChange(function () {
      window.ducter.get("landed").then(function (v) {
        landed = v || {};
        render();
      });
    });
  } else {
    render();
  }
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"items":[{"id":"ducter-412","title":"Compaction cut","repo":"wey-gu/ducter #412","waiting":"Waiting on Weizhen's review since Monday","status":"Review"},{"id":"mem-10287","title":"Retry on 502","repo":"nowledge-co/mem #10287","waiting":"CI is failing","status":"CI"},{"id":"hawdb-1592","title":"Flaky compaction test","repo":"nowledge-co/hawdb #1592","waiting":"Waiting on Alice since Tuesday","status":"Review"},{"id":"ducter-418","title":"Apps bar","repo":"wey-gu/ducter #418","waiting":"Still a draft","status":"Draft"}]}

var cat="交通";
var $=function(id){return document.getElementById(id)};
document.querySelectorAll(".k-chip").forEach(function(b){b.onclick=function(){cat=b.dataset.c;document.querySelectorAll(".k-chip").forEach(function(c){c.setAttribute("aria-pressed",c===b?"true":"false")})}});
function yuan(n){return "¥"+Number(n).toLocaleString("zh-CN")}
function draw(){
  var xs=ducter.get("items")||[];
  var total=xs.reduce(function(s,x){return s+Number(x.amt||0)},0);
  $("total").innerHTML=xs.length?yuan(total):'<span class="k-none">还没记</span>';
  $("sub").textContent=xs.length?xs.length+" 笔":"记了就会算合计";
  var list=$("list");list.innerHTML="";
  xs.forEach(function(x){
    var r=document.createElement("div");r.className="k-row";
    var m=document.createElement("div");m.className="k-row-main";m.textContent=x.trip;
    var s=document.createElement("div");s.className="k-row-sub";s.textContent=x.cat||"";
    var n=document.createElement("div");n.className="k-row-sub";n.textContent=x.note||"";
    var v=document.createElement("div");v.className="k-row-value";v.textContent=yuan(x.amt);
    r.append(m,s,n,v);list.appendChild(r);
  });
  $("empty").style.display=xs.length?"none":"block";
  ducter.summary(xs.length?xs.length+" 笔,合计 "+yuan(total):"还没记");
}
$("save").onclick=function(){
  var amt=$("amt").value;if(!amt)return;
  var xs=ducter.get("items")||[];
  xs.push({trip:$("trip").value.trim()||"出差",amt:Number(amt),cat:cat,day:new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10)});
  ducter.set("items",xs);$("amt").value="";draw();
};
ducter.onChange(draw);
draw();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"items":[{"trip":"北京出差 11/20–21","amt":662,"cat":"交通","day":"2026-11-20","note":"G1 07:00 上海虹桥→北京南"},{"trip":"北京出差 11/20–21","amt":1036,"cat":"住宿","day":"2026-11-20","note":"亚朵酒店（望京）两晚"}]}
  [also used tools] app
```

### picture-question-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 图里这是什么？适合放在空状态里吗？ (with a picture: dev_laptop_awake.webp)
  [partner message] 这是一个奶油色的 3D 风格笔记本电脑插画，屏幕是空白的，边框是深藏青色，背景透明。

适合放在空状态里。屏幕空白正好表达"还没有内容"，颜色柔和，不会显得冷冰冰。配上一句提示文字和一个按钮（比如"新建第一个…"），效果会更好。

需要注意两点：屏幕是空的，单看图不知道缺的是什么，所以文字要把意思说清楚。另外，深色模式下奶油色主体可能很亮，最好测一下对比度。
```
